    // (undocumented)
    createdAt: Time;
    // (undocumented)
    digest: Digest;
    executionRefs: EntityRef[];
    // (undocumented)
    expiresAt: Time;
    // (undocumented)
    profileRef: EntityRef;
    // (undocumented)
    receiptRef: CommandRef;
    // (undocumented)
    requestedBy: Actor;
    // (undocumented)
    resourceOrganizationId: UUID;
    // (undocumented)
    resultRef?: EntityRef;
    // (undocumented)
    retryOfRef?: EntityRef;
    // (undocumented)
    runRef: {
        type: "abh.evaluation-run";
        id: UUID;
        version: Version;
    };
    // (undocumented)
    seed: number;
    // (undocumented)
    status: "Queued" | "Completed" | "Inconclusive";
}

// @public (undocumented)
export interface EvaluationUncertainty {
    // (undocumented)
    confidenceLevel: 0.8 | 0.9 | 0.95 | 0.99;
    // (undocumented)
    estimate: number;
    // (undocumented)
    lowerBound: number;
    // (undocumented)
    method: "WilsonScore";
    // (undocumented)
    metric: RegisteredName;
    // (undocumented)
    sampleCount: number;
    // (undocumented)
    upperBound: number;
}

// @public (undocumented)
export interface EventChangeSummary {
    changedFields: string[];
    factRefs: EntityRef[];
}
